- **pdca-cycle**: Use when the user wants to run a lightweight continuous-improvement loop using PDCA (Plan, Do, Check, Act), the Deming cycle. - **pdca-cycle-trace**: Use this skill when the user invokes /pdca-cycle-trace or asks for a default public reasoning trace using PDCA Cycle.
